17: /**
18:  * Best-effort, append-only FIX-154 evidence. It snapshots values then writes
19:  * on a bounded dedicated executor: an audit insert never nests a database
20:  * connection inside an execution transaction and never changes a BUY result.
21:  */
22: @Slf4j
23: @Component
24: public class BuyHtfAssessmentAuditStore {
25:     private final JdbcTemplate jdbc;
26:     private final Executor executor;
27:     private final Fix154Metrics metrics;
28:     @Autowired(required = false)
29:     private ExecutionReplayScope replayScope;
30: 
31:     public BuyHtfAssessmentAuditStore(JdbcTemplate jdbc, @Qualifier("fix154AuditExecutor") Executor executor,
32:                                       Fix154Metrics metrics) {
33:         this.jdbc = jdbc;
34:         this.executor = executor;
35:         this.metrics = metrics;
36:     }
37: 
38:     public void record(TradeSignal reference, String path,
39:                        HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment,
40:                        String finalResult, String reason) {
41:         // Replay signals are JDBC-generated and have no entity id. Replay parity
42:         // uses its own evidence tables; never write a misleading Production audit.
43:         if (reference == null || assessment == null || reference.getId() == null
44:                 || (replayScope != null && replayScope.active())) return;
45:         AuditPayload payload = AuditPayload.from(reference, path, assessment, finalResult, reason);
46:         try {
47:             executor.execute(() -> persist(payload));
48:         } catch (RejectedExecutionException rejected) {
49:             metrics.auditWriteFailed();
50:             log.warn("FIX-154 assessment audit queue is full; BUY decision remains unchanged: path={}, referenceSignalId={}",
51:                     path, reference.getId());
52:         }
53:     }
54: 
55:     private void persist(AuditPayload p) {
56:         try {
57:             jdbc.update("""
58:                     INSERT INTO buy_htf_assessment_audit (
59:                       reference_signal_id, evaluation_path, evaluated_at, replay_mode,
60:                       one_hour_signal_id, one_hour_raw_decision, one_hour_effective_decision,
61:                       one_hour_candle_close_time, one_hour_age_seconds, one_hour_data_quality_valid, one_hour_usability_reason,
62:                       five_minute_signal_id, five_minute_raw_decision, five_minute_effective_decision,
63:                       five_minute_candle_close_time, five_minute_age_seconds, five_minute_data_quality_valid, five_minute_usability_reason,
64:                       confidence_threshold, assessment_band, hard_confirmation_present, max_initial_percent,
65:                       legacy_veto_result, final_result, final_reason)
66:                     VALUES (?, ?, ?, FALSE, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
67:                     """,
68:                     p.referenceId, p.path, Timestamp.from(p.evaluatedAt),
69:                     p.one.id, p.one.decision, p.effectiveOne, timestamp(p.one.candleClose), p.one.ageSeconds, p.one.quality, p.one.usability,
70:                     p.five.id, p.five.decision, p.effectiveFive, timestamp(p.five.candleClose), p.five.ageSeconds, p.five.quality, p.five.usability,
71:                     HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.HARD_CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD, p.band, p.hardConfirmationPresent, p.maxInitialPercent,
72:                     p.legacyVeto, p.finalResult, p.reason);
73:         } catch (Exception e) {
74:             metrics.auditWriteFailed();
75:             log.warn("FIX-154 assessment audit was not recorded; BUY decision remains unchanged: path={}, referenceSignalId={}",
76:                     p.path, p.referenceId, e);
77:         }
78:     }
79: 
80:     private static Timestamp timestamp(Instant value) { return value == null ? null : Timestamp.from(value); }
81: 
82:     private record Context(Long id, String decision, Instant candleClose, Long ageSeconds, Boolean quality, String usability) {
83:         static Context from(TradeSignal signal, Instant evaluatedAt) {
84:             if (signal == null) return new Context(null, null, null, null, null, "MISSING");
85:             Instant close = deriveCandleClose(signal);
86:             Long age = close == null ? null : Math.max(0L, Duration.between(close, evaluatedAt).getSeconds());
87:             return new Context(signal.getId(), signal.getDecision() == null ? null : signal.getDecision().name(), close, age,
88:                     signal.getDataQualityValid(), Boolean.TRUE.equals(signal.getDataQualityValid()) ? "AVAILABLE" : "QUALITY_UNKNOWN");
89:         }
90:     }
91:     private record AuditPayload(Long referenceId, String path, Instant evaluatedAt, Context one, Context five,
92:                                 String effectiveOne, String effectiveFive, String band, boolean hardConfirmationPresent,
93:                                 Integer maxInitialPercent, String legacyVeto, String finalResult, String reason) {
94:         static AuditPayload from(TradeSignal reference, String path, HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment,
95:                                  String finalResult, String reason) {
96:             Instant evaluated = reference.getGeneratedAt() == null ? Instant.now() : reference.getGeneratedAt();
97:             String legacy = "5M_BEARISH_VETO".equals(assessment.code()) || "1H_BEARISH_VETO".equals(assessment.code())
98:                     ? assessment.code() : null;
99:             return new AuditPayload(reference.getId(), path, evaluated,
100:                     Context.from(assessment.rawOneHour(), evaluated), Context.from(assessment.rawFiveMinute(), evaluated),

12:     private final AtomicLong auditWriteFailures = new AtomicLong();

14:     private final AtomicLong softAddBlocks = new AtomicLong();
15:

17:     public void auditWriteFailed() { auditWriteFailures.incrementAndGet(); }
18:     public void softCappedEntry() { softCappedEntries.incrementAndGet(); }

22:                 "audit_write_failure_count", auditWriteFailures.get(),
23:                 "soft_capped_entry_count", softCappedEntries.get(),
24:                 "soft_add_blocked_count", softAddBlocks.get());
25:     }

210:      * FIX-120 correction/refinement to FIX-118. Called in the same transaction that persists the new TP.
211:      * A TP change invalidates the prior executable lock as a calculation baseline. If current
212:      * best progress remains at/above activation, stay ACTIVE but derive a fresh lock from the
213:      * new geometry using raw extensionPrice as current and extensionHighest as the historical
214:      * best seed. If progress falls below activation, preserve the historical lock for audit but
215:      * make it non-executable in TP_EXTENSION_REBASE until the current geometry requalifies.
216:      */
217:     public ExtensionTransition onTakeProfitExtended(WalletManagedPosition position, BigDecimal newTarget, BigDecimal extensionPrice, Instant changedAt) {
218:         if (position == null || newTarget == null || position.getAverageEntryPriceUsdt() == null) {
219:             return ExtensionTransition.none();
220:         }
221:         ProfitLockState previousState = normalizedState(position);
222:         BigDecimal oldTarget = position.getTakeProfitUsdt();
223:         BigDecimal oldLock = position.getProfitLockPriceUsdt();
224:         BigDecimal extensionHighest = position.getHighestPriceUsdt() == null
225:                 ? position.getAverageEntryPriceUsdt()
226:                 : position.getHighestPriceUsdt();
227:         if (extensionPrice != null && extensionPrice.compareTo(extensionHighest) > 0) {
228:             extensionHighest = extensionPrice;
229:             position.setHighestPriceUsdt(extensionHighest);
230:         }
231:         BigDecimal progress = progressPercent(position.getAverageEntryPriceUsdt(), newTarget, extensionHighest);
232:         BigDecimal activation = configuredProfile(settings()).activationPercent();
233:         ProfitLockState nextState = previousState;
234:         // TP changed now, so persist progress against the new geometry immediately even though
235:         // the extension tick returns before the next normal Profit Lock evaluation.
236:         position.setProfitLockProgressPercent(progress);
237: 
238:         if (previousState == ProfitLockState.ACTIVE) {
239:             if (progress.compareTo(activation) < 0) {
240:                 nextState = ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE;
241:                 position.setProfitLockState(nextState);
242:                 position.setProfitLockRebaseStartedAt(changedAt == null ? Instant.now() : changedAt);
243:                 // Legacy compatibility flag deliberately stays true; ProfitLockState is the sole
244:                 // authority for whether the persisted historical lock may execute.
245:                 position.setProfitLockActive(true);
246:             } else {
247:                 // FIX-120: the extension still leaves the winner beyond activation, but the old
248:                 // lock belongs to the superseded TP geometry. Establish a fresh geometry baseline.
249:                 // Deliberately pass raw extensionPrice as current and extensionHighest as previous
250:                 // highest: ProfitLockPolicy then sees the real boundary tick plus the authoritative
251:                 // best price, while previousActive/previousLock are intentionally discarded.
252:                 ProfitLockProfile profile = configuredProfile(settings());
253:                 BigDecimal currentForPolicy = extensionPrice == null ? extensionHighest : extensionPrice;
254:                 ProfitLockPolicy.State rebased = profitLockPolicy.evaluate(
255:                         position.getAverageEntryPriceUsdt(), newTarget, currentForPolicy, extensionHighest,
256:                         false, null, true, profile.activationPercent(), profile.initialLockPercent(), profile.trailStepPercent());
257:                 nextState = rebased.active() ? ProfitLockState.ACTIVE : ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE;
258:                 position.setHighestPriceUsdt(rebased.highestPrice());
259:                 position.setProfitLockProgressPercent(rebased.progressPercent());
260:                 position.setProfitLockPriceUsdt(rebased.lockPrice());
261:                 position.setProfitLockState(nextState);
262:                 position.setProfitLockActive(true);
263:                 position.setProfitLockRebaseStartedAt(nextState == ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE
264:                         ? (changedAt == null ? Instant.now() : changedAt)
265:                         : null);
266:                 progress = rebased.progressPercent();
267:             }
268:         } else if (previousState == ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE) {
269:             // Repeated TP extensions keep the rebase active and refresh progress against the
270:             // newest geometry without resetting the original rebase start timestamp.
271:         }

package com.crypto.regression.replay;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.Instant;

/**
 * FIX-156: immutable, as-of policy evidence consumed by Replay only.
 * Production continues to read its live configuration through its own paths.
 */
public record ReplayPolicyProfile(
        long runId,
        String profileHash,
        Instant effectiveAt,
        boolean dynamicProfitLockEnabled,
        BigDecimal profitLockActivationPercent,
        BigDecimal profitLockInitialPercent,
        BigDecimal profitLockTrailStepPercent,
        BigDecimal fixedNotionalUsdt) {

    public ReplayPolicyProfile {
        if (profileHash == null || profileHash.isBlank()) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("FIX-156 Replay policy profile hash is required");
        }
        if (effectiveAt == null || fixedNotionalUsdt == null || fixedNotionalUsdt.signum() <= 0) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("FIX-156 Replay policy profile is incomplete");
        }
    }
}
